language: java

new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when null is passed as a value during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithNullValue
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNullValue() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat.DEFAULT.printRecord(writer, "Value1", null, "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains(CSVFormat.DEFAULT.getNullString()));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a quote character is set and a value contains it.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithQuoteCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithQuoteCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Quote\"Value", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Quote\"\"Value\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the escape character is set and a value contains special characters.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Special\\Value", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Special\\\\Value"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when trimming is enabled and values have surrounding spaces.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true);
          format.printRecord(writer, "  Value1  ", "  Value2  ");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.startsWith("  "));
          assertFalse(output.endsWith("  "));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the header comments are set and printed.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithHeaderComments
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithHeaderComments() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withHeaderComments("Comment 1", "Comment 2");
          format.print(writer);
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Comment 1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Comment 2"));
      }

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of special characters in CSV values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include special characters (e.g., CR, LF, delimiter).
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the special character handling branch is taken.
      3. The output is generated with proper escaping for special characters.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes strings with CR, LF, and the configured delimiter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes special characters and maintains the intended CSV format.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when quote mode is set to NON_NUMERIC.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withQuoteMode(QuoteMode quoteModePolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withQuoteMode() with QuoteMode.NON_NUMERIC.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for non-numeric values is executed.
      3. The output is generated based on the quote mode policy.
    input_focus: |
      Set the quote mode to NON_NUMERIC and provide a mix of numeric and non-numeric values in printRecord().
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that non-numeric values are quoted while numeric values are not, ensuring correct CSV formatting.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of empty records when ignoring empty lines.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean getIgnoreEmptyLines()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVParser parse(Reader in)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call parse() with a Reader containing multiple empty lines.
      2. During parsing, getIgnoreEmptyLines() is reached and the branch for ignoring empty lines is executed.
      3. The parser processes the input and skips empty lines.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a Reader that contains multiple consecutive empty lines interspersed with valid CSV records.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the resulting parsed records do not include any empty records and reflect only valid data.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when skipping the header record.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean getSkipHeaderRecord()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVParser parse(Reader in)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call parse() with a Reader containing a header followed by data records.
      2. During parsing, getSkipHeaderRecord() is reached and the branch for skipping the header is executed.
      3. The parser processes the input and skips the first record.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a Reader with a header line followed by multiple data lines, and configure to skip the header.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the parsed records start from the first data line and do not include the header in the output.

  - design_name: |
      Test validation of duplicate headers in CSV format.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with an array containing duplicate header names.
      2. During validation, validate() is reached and the branch for duplicate headers is executed.
      3. An exception is thrown due to the presence of duplicate headers.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of header names that includes duplicates when calling withHeader().
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating the presence of duplicate header entries.

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintRecordWithNullValue() throws IOException
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test handling of special characters in CSV values.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withDelimiter(',');
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value\n2", "Value,3", "Value\r4");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\n2"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value,3"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\r4"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when quote mode is set to NON_NUMERIC.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithQuoteModeNonNumeric
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithQuoteModeNonNumeric() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.NON_NUMERIC);
          format.printRecord(writer, 123, "Text", 456.78);
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.contains("123"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Text\""));
          assertFalse(output.contains("456.78"));
      }
    new_imports_code: 'import org.apache.commons.csv.QuoteMode;'

  - test_behavior: Test handling of empty records when ignoring empty lines.
    test_name: testParseWithEmptyLines
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithEmptyLines() throws IOException {
          StringReader reader = new StringReader("Col1,Col2\n\nValue1,Value2\n\n\nValue3,Value4");
          CSVParser parser = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreEmptyLines(true).parse(reader);
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(3, records.size());
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0));
          assertEquals("Value2", records.get(0).get(1));
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0));
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1));
      }
    new_imports_code: 'import java.io.StringReader;'

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when skipping the header record.
    test_name: testParseWithSkipHeaderRecord
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithSkipHeaderRecord() throws IOException {
          StringReader reader = new StringReader("Header1,Header2\nValue1,Value2\nValue3,Value4");
          CSVParser parser = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withSkipHeaderRecord(true).parse(reader);
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size());
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0));
          assertEquals("Value2", records.get(0).get(1));
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0));
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1));
      }
    new_imports_code: 'import java.io.StringReader;'

  - test_behavior: Test validation of duplicate headers in CSV format.
    test_name: testWithHeaderDuplicateEntries
    test_code: |
      @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
      public void testWithHeaderDuplicateEntries() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withHeader("Col1", "Col1");
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a character is less than or equal to space.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that contains a character less than or equal to space.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the branch checking for characters is executed.
      3. The method processes the character accordingly based on the condition.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes a character such as a tab or a space to trigger the specific branch.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output handles the character correctly, ensuring proper escaping or formatting as defined by the logic.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the quote mode is not set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that requires quoting but has no quote mode set.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for handling quote mode is executed.
      3. The method processes the value without applying any quotes.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that would normally require quoting but ensure that the quote mode is set to NONE.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output reflects the original value without any quotes being applied.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a trailing delimiter is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a set of values and ensure the trailing delimiter is configured.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the branch for handling trailing delimiters is executed.
      3. The method appends the trailing delimiter to the output.
    input_focus: |
      Set up a CSVFormat instance with trailing delimiters enabled and pass it to printRecord().
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output includes the trailing delimiter as specified in the format configuration.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to null.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a set of values and ensure the record separator is set to null.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the branch checking for a null record separator is executed.
      3. The method processes the output without appending any record separator.
    input_focus: |
      Configure a CSVFormat instance with a null record separator and pass it to printRecord().
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output does not include any record separator, reflecting the null configuration.

      Test behavior when the escape character is not set.

      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)

        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)

      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the branch for handling the absence of an escape character is executed.
      3. The method processes the value without applying any escape logic.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that contains special characters while ensuring the escape character is not set.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output reflects the original value without any escaping applied.

          Mockito.when(mockMetaData.getColumnLabel(2)).thenReturn("Col2");

          assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"Col1", "Col2"}, format.getHeader());

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when escape character is set and special characters are present.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacterAndSpecialChars
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacterAndSpecialChars() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value\\2", "Value\n3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\\2"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\n3"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when quote mode is set to NON_NUMERIC.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithNonNumericQuoteMode
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNonNumericQuoteMode() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.NON_NUMERIC);
          format.printRecord(writer, 123, "StringValue");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.contains("\"123\"")); // Numeric value should not be quoted
          assertTrue(output.contains("StringValue")); // String value should be quoted
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when quote mode is set to ALL.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithAllQuoteMode

          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.ALL);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value with spaces", "Value, with comma");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.startsWith("\"")); // First value should be quoted
          assertTrue(output.endsWith("\"")); // Last value should be quoted
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.startsWith("  "));
          assertFalse(output.endsWith("  "));
      }

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a new record is printed with a trailing delimiter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a set of values and a valid Appendable.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the trailing delimiter condition is evaluated.
      3. The path for adding a trailing delimiter is taken based on the configuration.
    input_focus: |
      Use a CSVFormat instance with trailingDelimiter set to true and provide a non-empty array of values.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output includes the specified delimiter followed by the record separator.

  - design_name: |
      Test escaping behavior when special characters are present in values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value containing special characters that require escaping.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the escape character condition is evaluated.
      3. The path for escaping special characters is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes characters like newline, carriage return, or the delimiter itself.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to the defined escape character.

  - design_name: |
      Test quoting behavior for values that require encapsulation.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that necessitates quoting due to special characters.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the quoting condition is evaluated.
      3. The path for encapsulating the value in quotes is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Use a value that contains characters like the quote character itself or the delimiter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output encapsulates the value in quotes and handles any internal quotes correctly.

  - design_name: |
      Test comment marker behavior when comments are present in the input.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isCommentMarkerSet()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVParser parse(final Reader in)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call parse() with a Reader containing lines that start with a comment marker.
      2. During parsing, isCommentMarkerSet() is reached to check if comments are enabled.
      3. The path for processing comment lines is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a Reader with lines that include a comment marker followed by comment text.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the parser correctly identifies and ignores the comment lines during processing.

new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a new record is printed with a trailing delimiter.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter(true);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.endsWith(format.getDelimiter() + format.getRecordSeparator()));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test escaping behavior when special characters are present in values.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharactersEscaping
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharactersEscaping() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          format.print(writer, "Value1", writer, true);
          format.print(writer, "Value\n2", writer, true);
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\\n2"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test quoting behavior for values that require encapsulation.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithQuoting
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithQuoting() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value,2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value,2\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test comment marker behavior when comments are present in the input.
    test_name: testParseWithCommentMarker
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithCommentMarker() throws IOException {
          String input = "# This is a comment\nValue1,Value2\n# Another comment\nValue3,Value4";
          Reader reader = new StringReader(input);
          CSVParser parser = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withCommentMarker('#').parse(reader);
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Only two records should be parsed
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testWithHeaderNull()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when header case sensitivity is ignored.
    test_name: testWithHeaderCaseSensitivityIgnored
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithHeaderCaseSensitivityIgnored() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreHeaderCase(true);
          format = format.withHeader("Name", "name");
          String[] headers = format.getHeader();
          assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"Name", "name"}, headers);
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test handling of header comments in CSV format.
    test_name: testWithHeaderComments
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithHeaderComments() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withHeaderComments("Generated by XYZ");
          String[] comments = format.getHeaderComments();
          assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"Generated by XYZ"}, comments);
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test trimming behavior for leading and trailing spaces.
    test_name: testTrimLeadingAndTrailingSpaces
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testTrimLeadingAndTrailingSpaces() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true);
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "  value1  ", "  value2  ");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.startsWith("  "));
          assertFalse(output.endsWith("  "));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when no escape character is set with QuoteMode.NONE.
    test_name: testNoEscapeCharacterWithQuoteModeNone
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testNoEscapeCharacterWithQuoteModeNone() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(null).withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.NONE);
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1,Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a quote character is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(Object value, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out) throws IOException
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that contains special characters and a set quote character.
      2. During printing, print(...) is reached and the branch for handling the quote character is taken.
      3. The output is formatted with the specified quote character around the value.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes a comma and set a quote character (e.g., '"').
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output includes the value encapsulated in the specified quote character.

  - design_name: |
      Test escape character handling for special characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out) throws IOException
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that includes escape characters and a set escape character.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the branch for handling escape characters is taken.
      3. The output is formatted with the escape character applied to special characters.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes a newline character and set an escape character (e.g., '\\').
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to the escape character rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test trimming behavior for leading and trailing spaces.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CharSequence trim(CharSequence charSequence)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withTrim(boolean trim)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withTrim() with the trim flag set to true.
      2. During formatting, trim(...) is reached and the branch for trimming spaces is taken.
      3. The output is generated with leading and trailing spaces removed from values.
    input_focus: |
      Set up a CSVFormat instance with values that have leading and trailing spaces.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output values do not contain any leading or trailing spaces.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(String recordSeparator)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out) throws IOException
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a custom record separator set.
      2. During printing, the custom record separator is applied when generating output.
      3. The output is formatted with the specified record separator between records.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with a custom record separator (e.g., "\n").
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output separates records using the specified custom record separator.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when allowing missing column names in the header.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat withAllowMissingColumnNames(boolean allowMissingColumnNames)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVParser parse(Reader in) throws IOException
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call parse() with a CSV input that has missing column names.
      2. During parsing, the branch for allowing missing column names is taken.
      3. The parser processes the input without throwing an exception for missing headers.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a CSV input stream that lacks a header row.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the parser successfully processes the input and handles missing column names gracefully.

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintRecordWithQuoteCharacter()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a quote character is set.
    test_name: testPrintWithQuoteCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintWithQuoteCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.print(writer, "Value, with comma", false);
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value, with comma\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test escape character handling for special characters.
    test_name: testPrintWithEscapeCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintWithEscapeCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          format.print(writer, "Value with newline\nand tab\tcharacter", false);
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value with newline\\n"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("and tab\\tcharacter"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test trimming behavior for leading and trailing spaces.
    test_name: testTrimLeadingAndTrailingSpaces
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testTrimLeadingAndTrailingSpaces() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrim(true);
          format.printRecord(writer, "  Value1  ", "  Value2  ");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertFalse(output.startsWith("  "));
          assertFalse(output.endsWith("  "));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    test_name: testCustomRecordSeparator
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withRecordSeparator("\n");
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\n"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when allowing missing column names in the header.
    test_name: testAllowMissingColumnNames
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testAllowMissingColumnNames() throws IOException {
          String input = "Value1,Value2\nValue3,Value4";
          Reader reader = new StringReader(input);
          CSVParser parser = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withAllowMissingColumnNames().parse(reader);
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Two records should be parsed
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
